# Changelog

What changed in the Architect Workbench, one entry per week (ISO week, Monday to Sunday), newest first. The form
follows [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/). Customers and projects appear as codes only.

## Week 48, 2026-11-23 to 2026-11-29 (in progress)
### Added
- B-097: a tenant is named `tcp-NNNNN` (five or six digits, `codes.TENANT`) and `test-NNNNN` is its old name for a window of 30 days from the line `window-from DATE` of `rules/tenant-alias-window.txt` (2026-11-27, so until 2026-12-27): `awb/tenantname.py` (`canonical`, `twin`, `window_end`) prints one line "test-NNNNN is the old name of tcp-NNNNN, until DATE" inside the window and refuses the old name after it (also with no file or a bad date). `tenants.get` and `tenants.resolve` take either name for a tenant of the register, `awb tenant now|history|at|snapshot|tags` and `awb tenant cost note` too; the key service maps a request to the alias keys.conf holds and the lab admin list takes either name (`labadmin.allowed`); `--tenant` of `awb cloud call`, `awb lab`, `awb lab run`, `awb lab obs`, `awb kb recheck-run`, `awb kb recheck-watch` and `awb spawn` is read the same way. A tenant alias is never a project code and the reverse: `tcp-q7m4` and `tcp-1049` are no alias of any register, of keys.conf, of `tenant_write`, of the file tidy or of `awb spawn --tenant` (`codes.NOT_A_PROJECT`). The dataset scrub reads `tcp-NNNNN` as a tenant like `test-NNNNN`. Work rules and README name `tcp-NNNNN`. Tests: `tests/test_tenant_alias_synonyms.py`; README.
- B-108: a held stop names its source. `ux.hook_event` takes a `text` for the stop hook: the fixed tokens of the messages that held it, joined by "+" (`ux.HELD_TOKENS`: ledger, harvest, contrib, late, drafts, open, next-step, and lessons for the lesson ask, which held too and had no token in the task's list); `hook_stop` passes them in the order of its messages and a development session's next-step hold passes `next-step`. A token outside the list is refused (`UxError`); a prompt or compaction line keeps "-". `awb ux report` prints one row "held by TOKEN: N" per token under the table. Tests: `tests/test_ux_held_source.py`; README.
- B-099: the stop hook of a development session (a checkout of this repository) holds a reply once when its last paragraph names the session's own next step (`hooks.NEXT_STEP_PHRASES`: "следующее по плану", "теперь чиню", "начинаю с", "жду проверку", "next:", "i will now" and the like, whole words, case ignored) and the reply names no step for him (no line "Твои шаги:" or "Your steps:" with text other than "нет" or "none"). One line on stderr: do the step now, wait on a build task or check with `awb build wait ID` in the background and end the turn only on a step that is his. The ux event is `held-note`; `stop_hook_active` lets the next stop through; project and desk sessions keep their path. Tests: `tests/test_dev_stop_next_step.py`; README.
- B-096, lesson producers L2 L3 L4 (DESIGN section 8, awb/lessons.py): the stop hook of a project session asks once per batch (the harvest pattern, a mark per session in `<shared>/sessions/lessons-<session id>.json`) "write what you learned as lesson cards or say that there is nothing" after a shell step that failed and then worked (the same program and subcommand), a reverted step (`git revert`, `git reset --hard`, `git restore`, `git checkout --`) or a new line under `## Decisions` of STATE.md; a lesson card written after the step answers it. The PreCompact hook of a project leaves a mark and the first prompt after the compaction asks the same question once. Neither asks a side session or an ask project. `awb lessons distill CODE`, run as the work user in the project folder, reads only the transcripts of that project's sessions of the work user (the client folders named after the project folder, a folder inside it or a side session; never another project, the owner or the desk), takes the episodes it has not read before (a step that failed and then worked with its first error line, a reverted step, new decision lines, each with what the session said next), keeps a word only when it is plain English or a TCP term and writes [x] for every other (no name, project code, identifier, home path, secret or price; every line passes the screens of `kb add`), writes `notes/lessons-digest.md` and starts `awb side start lessons` with it on haiku at effort low, which turns it into lesson cards. Nothing new starts nothing. `awb close` runs the distill once more before it closes a live project and never stops the close for it.
- B-095, lesson cards L1 and L5 (Q-0020, DESIGN section 8): `awb kb pattern add --kind lesson [--sub dead-end|working-path|gotcha|decision] [--why TEXT]` writes a lesson card with the header keys `sub`, `grade` (always `said`) and `until` (its date plus 90 days); `--sub` is the recommended form and a lesson written without it is a `gotcha`, which the command says in one line on stdout; a `decision` needs `--why` (stored as `reason`, checked like the statement) and no other sort takes it; `--sub` on another kind is refused. `awb kb pattern find` and `list` leave out a lesson past its best-before unless `--all` is given (`LAPSED DATE` marks it then). The knowledge base first block adds, after the facts, under the line "Learned in other projects (leads, not facts)", at most three lesson cards whose words match the prompt (two content words as for facts), never a card the project of the session wrote or supports, a lapsed or a retired one; their room in the 1500 characters is kept before the facts. The push to a project on the same topic is not part of this. Tests: `tests/test_lesson_cards.py`.
- B-106: the ended block of `awb owner status` keeps one line per kind and id, the latest end with its outcome (an item that ended needs-you, then red, then ok shows one ok line). Every line that is not ok is shown; of the ok lines it shows the newest `ENDED_OK_MAX = 5` and one line "and N more ended ok". `events.ended_rows` and the status file are unchanged. Tests: `tests/test_owner_ended_collapse.py`; README.
- B-101: `awb dev reopen HANDLE --error TEXT`, run in the asking project, takes a closed ask back to open: it records `reopened` (a count), `reopened_at` and `reopened_release` (the release it failed on), appends "## Reopened N" with the error and starts the ask again in the events file; an open ask and another project's ask are refused in one line. The "live in release X" note adds "Run the command you recorded again: CMD" when the ask has a command, and `awb dev status` shows "reopened N".
- B-103: `awb build add FILE` prints, after the task is queued, the line "tests that import the modules of paths:" with every file under `tests/` that has an `import` or `from` line of a module of the `paths` line (a plain scan, no run), and when `tests_replaced` is none one more line: "if the task changes a behaviour these tests assert, name them in tests_replaced". The queue entry is unchanged. The scan reads the working copy above `build/queue`; a scratch queue folder elsewhere prints neither line. Tests: `tests/test_build_add_tests_hint.py`.
- B-100: `awb dev ask TEXT --run RUN [--call N]` attaches the failing lab call to the ask: it reads call N of the run log of the project (default: the last call with a status of 400 or more) through `lablog.read_call` and adds a `## Call` section with the method, the service, the path with every id as `{id}`, the keys of the request body (no value) and the answer status, `error_code`, `error_msg`, `code` and `message`. Every value passes `screen`; a value that fails is dropped with its key named. A run that is not of this project or a missing call is refused in one line. Tests: `tests/test_dev_ask_run.py`; README and the commands guide.
- B-105: a deploy waits for a running winlab transfer. `awb winlab get` and `put` write a start (events kind `transfer`, id `MACHINE@PID`, the project code) and an end with `ok`, `red` or `stopped`; a transfer that is killed leaves its start, which `running_now` shows. `sudo awb deploy` refuses in one line and exit 2 ("a winlab transfer runs for tcp-xxxx since HH:MM; deploy after it ends or with --force") while a transfer of the last 6 hours has no end and its pid lives; `--force` deploys anyway; a start with a dead pid is stale, the deploy says so and goes on. A dry run and a host without a shared side are not held. The kind `transfer` is in the status file and the API contract (`docs/api/openapi.yaml`). Test: `tests/test_deploy_winlab_transfer.py`.
- B-085, DMZ 1, tcp-x7k2 ask 2026-11-25-tcp-x7k2-1 (the owner's word of 2026-11-25, "Согласен полностью": the entry keeps registered names and real secrets only): the entry of customer material (`awb import` with `--trusted`, `--paste`, the bucket folder `in/`, `awb start --from`, `awb inbox take`, `awb paste`, `awb material add`, the console import and task description) keeps IP addresses (v4 and v6), host names and web addresses (the platform's endpoints included), hashes, UUIDs and tenant, domain or project numbers as written, no finding and no token (`patterns.ENTRY_KEPT`, `_Engine(entry=True)`, `check.check_text(..., entry=True)`, `hooks.name_hits(..., entry=True)`, `check.check_file(..., entry=True)`); registered names stay codes, mail addresses stay a finding, real secrets stay `[secret]`. A technical word is never a person or a place: an acronym or its plural (`VMs`, `IPs`, `ECSs`, `VPCs`, `SGs`), a hyphen compound in lower case (`pre-shared-key`) and the words of `rules/allowed-terms.txt` (the new ones listed there). A script line that only reads or sets a variable (`pm.environment.set("token", pm.response.json().access_token)`, `{{token}}`, `$env:TOKEN`, `$(call)`) is no secret, a literal in such a call is. The tool guard reads the pattern of a grep, egrep, fgrep or rg as text, so a URL or a regular expression with slashes is no path to another project; a real path outside the project is still refused. The laptop clean and every exit check are not touched. Tests: `tests/test_dmz_entry.py`.
- tcp-bytg ask 2026-11-25-tcp-bytg-6 (the owner's word of 2026-11-25: no IAM agency, no extra lab keys), part 1: the key service fills `{{secret:obs-ak}}` and `{{secret:obs-sk}}` with the key the call itself uses, only inside `server.user_data_text` of a POST on ECS cloudservers (v1 and v1.1); it sends the filled text as base64 `user_data` and logs the call as "user data with the tenant key"; the two names are refused anywhere else; every answer of `awb cloud call` leaves out the user data of a server (`user_data`, `OS-EXT-SRV-ATTR:user_data`). The recipe `disk-reader` still uses the agency until part 2.
- tcp-bytg ask 2026-11-25-tcp-bytg-6 part 2 (the owner's word of 2026-11-25: no agency): the recipe `disk-reader` has no param `agency` any more; its reader ECS gets the script as `user_data_text` (`${usertext:diskreader}`) and `awb/tcp/diskreader.py` signs its puts with the key of the tenant, filled in by the key service, not with an agency credential from the metadata service. The lab runs (`lab_call`) fill `{{secret:obs-ak}}` and `{{secret:obs-sk}}` with the lab key of the tenant (the write key of a customer tenant), log the call as "user data with the tenant key" and show a note of the length in place of the user data of a server in every answer; the run does not ask the key service for the two names (`lab.secrets_of`). The three tests of tests/test_recipe_disk_reader.py written for the agency design keep their names and check the new one.
- B-083, tcp-bytg ask 2026-11-21-tcp-bytg-12: `awb lab run end --keep NAME[,NAME...]` ends the run and deletes everything except the named OBS buckets, which go into the kept list of the project (tenant, region, kind, name, run, date) and keep the run's tags; any other kind in `--keep` is refused before a delete; `awb lab run status` and `awb owner status` show the list and `awb lab kept drop NAME` deletes a kept bucket later with the delete and proof of a run end; 3 tests in tests/test_lab_end_keep.py
- B-082, tcp-bytg ask 2026-11-21-tcp-bytg-22 part 1: `awb doc fetch` follows a redirect whose target carries a query string when the host is on the lists (at most 3 redirects as before); a redirect to a host off the lists stays refused with or without a query, and the first URL still takes no query. The page records the final URL with its query; the ledger keeps the path only. Tests: `tests/test_doc_redirect_query.py`; the redirect test of `tests/test_doc.py` now expects the redirect with a query followed.
- tcp-bytg ask 2026-11-21-tcp-bytg-10: the recipe `disk-reader` (`awb lab run disk-reader --param server_id=ID`; `awb lab list` shows it and `awb lab dry-run` its calls) reads Windows state of a stopped server (setupapi*.log, System.evtx, Application.evtx, the hives SYSTEM and SOFTWARE and the files of the parameter `files`): it refuses a server that is not SHUTOFF or not in its AZ before it makes anything, makes a bucket, a security group with no inbound rule and a small Linux reader ECS without an EIP in the subnet and AZ of the server, detaches the system volume, attaches it to the reader, which mounts it with ntfs-3g and `ro` and puts the files into the bucket, then attaches it back to the server with the same device name and deletes the reader. The reader script is `awb/tcp/diskreader.py` (the user_data: standard library, no secret, nothing from a public bucket; its puts are signed with the credential of the reader's IAM agency, parameter `agency`). The engine (`awb/tcp/lab.py`) gained: `restore`, which the teardown runs first, also after a failed step or a signal, once the detach step was reached and which proves the volume back with a read (a volume not proven back keeps the reader, writes one line for the owner and exits 4); `keep` on a create step (the bucket stays, its row says kept); a wait on the status of an answer (`until: {status: [200]}`); a preflight on one object; `${userdata:diskreader}`. Tests: `tests/test_recipe_disk_reader.py`; README, `recipes/README.md` and the commands guide describe it. The test `test_the_three_recipes_are_valid_tag_every_resource_and_use_known_tags` of `tests/test_lab.py` lists the recipe names and needs the new name in its list.
- tcp-bj3o ask 2026-11-25-tcp-bj3o-1: `labclient.call(..., scope="domain")` takes a GET to eps with a domain scoped token made for that call and never kept (`labadmin.DOMAIN_READ_SERVICES`); another method or service with that scope is refused with one line that names the list; the region eu-nl is refused on the lab tenants with "eu-nl is closed on the test tenants by the owner's rule"; a key with a security token (`obs.Keys(ak, sk, token)`, temporary AK/SK of assume_role or an agency) is signed with X-Security-Token (x-obs-security-token for obs) by `cloud.Client`; tests in tests/test_lab_domain_scope.py; README, commands guide
- tcp-bytg ask 2026-11-21-tcp-bytg-14: `awb winlab extend NAME --minutes N` (the open window ends N minutes from now, the limits of open); status, log and stop of a job that started inside a window work after the window ended (the broker serves only these then); `run` appends `| Out-String -Width 200` to a command with no Format or Out command; `put` and `get` take `--overwrite`; new `awb winlab wait JOB [--timeout S]` with backoff; printed output over 2000 lines is cut to the first and last 1000 with one line that says how many (the log keeps all); a missing guest credential file gives one plain line; run, transfer and job records carry start and end time; tests in tests/test_winlab_gaps.py and tests/test_winlab_gaps_broker.py; README, commands guide
- tcp-bytg ask 2026-11-21-tcp-bytg-21: the intake keeps a code file (.ps1 .psm1 .psd1 .sh .py .bat .cmd .tf .yaml .yml .json .sql) whole: names, secrets and addresses are replaced in its string literals and comments only, the code between them arrives byte for byte and no identifier, property name or variable is read as a URL or a bank code (a hex or base64 run is still decoded and checked); a Word file with tracked changes gets a `### Tracked changes` list with each insertion and deletion, its author number, text and the 40 characters before it; tests in tests/test_intake_code_files.py; README, commands guide
- tcp-bytg ask 2026-11-21-tcp-bytg-20: the start hook shows STATE.md, OPEN.md and the pinned block when their only structured data is a reserved test domain (example, test, invalid, localhost, local and their suffixes) or a private address (10/8, 172.16/12, 192.168/16, 100.64/10, fd00::/8); a public address or a real looking domain is still withheld. New `awb state rotate [--keep N]` (module `awb/state.py`) moves the dated layers older than the last N dates to `history/STATE-YYYY-MM-DD.md` and writes the Pinned block from the lines marked `pinned:`. Tests: `tests/test_state_rotate.py`.
- tcp-bytg ask 2026-11-21-tcp-bytg-17: the tool guard reads the list of a `for VAR in ITEM...; do ...; done` loop: plain relative paths pass when the body passes for one path; an item that is a variable, a substitution, an absolute or home path or has `..` is refused. A glob item still passes (the honest-session test of tests/test_guard_review.py needs it); tests in tests/test_guard_literal_loops.py.
- Task 7 of the report design (the owner's yes of 2026-11-24): `awb report --codes` writes the KPI file `projects-kpi.csv` and `projects-kpi.meta.json` beside `projects.csv` by the same route (`--to-shared` on the work side, `--from-shared --put --if-changed` on the owner side, `reports/` of the owner bucket) for his reports and the half-year review: every project since 2026-09-01, closed and deleted ones too, ten columns (project code, customer code, kind, goal, start date, close date, state, tags and technologies, ledger entries, main deliverables), codes only, the goal, tags and deliverables through the check of `projects.csv`. `awb clean report` brings it to the laptop beside `projects.csv`. `projects.csv` and its six headers are unchanged; the timer units are unchanged. New module `awb/reportkpi.py`. Tests: `tests/test_report_kpi.py`.
- The project kind ask (the owner's wish of 2026-11-24): `awb start ask --goal "..."`, `awb spawn ask` and `awb projects kind CODE ask` make his standing question session on how TCP behaves. It never has a customer (the switch is refused with one or with anything in `input/`) and no material enters it: `awb import`, the paste door, `awb paste`, `awb material`, `awb inbox take`, `awb xchg`, `awb bucket pull` and `awb fetch` refuse it in one line, and the key service refuses its exchange ops. Its hooks check secrets only (prompt, post-write, the start's files, no draft gate or state reminders at the stop), the tool guard lets an unreadable shell path pass while every refused place still refuses, and the commit gate of its repository looks for secrets only. It reads the test tenants with the read role only: the lab role, a write, a lab run, `awb lab obs` and a customer tenant are refused by the client and by the key service. `awb kb add` runs every check there as everywhere. The kind comes from the owner's grant alone (kind ask and the folder's key token; the key service answers the new op `kind` for the work user's hooks); a row or a SCOPE.md that says ask only adds refusals. Its CLAUDE.md: facts first, then the public docs, then a read call; the source of each answer; a verified new finding into the facts with `awb kb add`; short answers. New module `awb/askkind.py`. Tests: `tests/test_ask_kind.py`.
- tcp-bytg ask 2026-11-21-19: `awb kb merge KEEP DROP [--why TEXT] [--source TEXT]` retires a duplicate into the entry that stays (reason `merged into KEEP`); KEEP takes the sources, tags and tried texts of both, `show DROP` and `find DROP` name KEEP, INDEX.md and the dataset build leave DROP out. `awb kb renew ID --source URL [--checked D]` records that a fact still holds where it was read again: today's check date, the new source, the grade kept. `awb kb expired --days N` adds the entries that expire in the next N days and the list ends with the commands to act on one. Each runs the checks of `amend`. Tests: `tests/test_bytg_kb_merge.py`.
- tcp-ui5o ask 2026-11-24-3: `awb docx FILE [--out DOCX] [--title T] [--h1-page]` writes a Word file of a Markdown or HTML deliverable for partners who edit with tracked changes, with the structure of its PDF and the standard library only: the parser and the folder, link, name and secret rules of `awb pdf`; headings in the styles Heading 1 to 3 with the colour of the page and a Word numbering when their numbers count up; a contents field at `<!-- toc -->` whose result already lists the headings; PNG and JPEG as inline pictures, an inline svg as an SVG picture with a PNG fallback drawn by pdftoppm; captions, tables with a repeated header row, code in the style Code, real Word lists, bookmarks and links to them, page / pages in the footer. `awb pdf` keeps the bytes of an image file for it. New module `awb/docx.py`. Tests: `tests/test_ui5o_docx.py`.
### Changed
- B-094, findings F6 and F7 of the attacker review of 2026-11-26: `awb lab obs link` is an exit. Before the key service signs, it reads the object (when it is 8 MiB or less) and runs the exit check of a public push on it: a registered name or a secret (secret, private key or token) refuses the link with one line that names the class and never the text, an id is no finding (the EXIT_FREE rule of B-087). An object over 8 MiB (an image for a reader) is not read. What the run's own disk reader wrote under `files/` and `manifest.json` (an object the run records no put of) is signed as before. `awb close` refuses while the project keeps a bucket of a lab run (the kept list of the vault; the work user asks the key service) with the line `awb lab kept drop NAME` for each; `--force` closes anyway. Tests: `tests/test_lab_link_exit.py` and `tests/test_close_kept_bucket.py`.
- B-092, findings F1 and F2 of the attacker review of 2026-11-26 (the owner's design of tcp-bytg-6 stays: the reader signs with the key of the call, no agency, no token): the key service no longer fills `{{secret:obs-ak}}` and `{{secret:obs-sk}}` into a text the caller wrote. A server create names the script and its parameters, `server.user_data_script` `{"name": "diskreader", "params": {"bucket", "region", "volume", "files"}}`; the service renders `awb/tcp/diskreader.py` itself (each parameter checked by its pattern, the bucket one of the project, the region the one of the call), fills the key and sends base64 `user_data`, on `awb cloud call` and on a lab run (`lab_call`). Another script name, an unknown parameter or a shell character in one is refused; a `user_data_text` takes no reference any more and a `user_data` that carries one of the two names, plain or in base64, is refused. The recipe `disk-reader` sends `${script:diskreader}` (the engine expands it to the name and the parameters) in place of `${usertext:diskreader}`, which is gone. F2: a lease of a tenant is refused while a server made with the tenant key in its user data may still be there (the service notes each such create by its id and name and asks the platform at the lease whether it is gone), since a leased key reads servers past the service; the lease policy is unchanged. The teardown of `disk-reader` deletes the reader first, right after the restore. Tests: `tests/test_keys_userdata_script.py`; the text-fill tests of `tests/test_keys_userdata_secret.py`, `tests/test_lab_userdata_secret.py` and `tests/test_recipe_disk_reader.py` keep their names and check the new behaviour.
- B-110: the README that `awb dataset build` writes for the public repository shimoza/tcp-facts opens with a first screen for a reader: one sentence what it is and for whom, the three ways to use it (attach facts.md, the one-click `tcp-facts.mcpb`, `claude mcp add`), "What this proves" (facts, facts tested live, services, weekly releases counted from the date tags of the local clone, each with its jq or gh command), one example fact, one freshness line (the facts with a recheck due and the jq command that lists them, the facts with a weekly automated check, level V4, and the check periods of the rest), the release date and the licence line. The table "Facts with a recheck due" is gone, the README no longer names PROMPT.md (only in the zip) and calls MANIFEST.json, HOW-TO.pdf and the .mcpb release assets, and no text says the dataset is rechecked weekly or "every 30 days". A contract fact whose source cites the current revision of the service description (or cites none and was checked on or after it) is no longer recheck due for its date while that revision is current; a requested recheck still makes it due. Exported source notes say "earlier private knowledge base" for "old setup". `tcp_facts_mcp.py` carries an Apache-2.0 header, the export and the repository get `LICENSE-CODE` (the Apache License 2.0 of this repository), MANIFEST.json gets `code_licence`; the facts stay CC BY 4.0. "What the facts cover" says how many facts name a certificate or an attestation and in which topics. Tests: `tests/test_dataset_readme_first_screen.py`; changed to the new layout, names kept: `tests/test_dataset.py` test_the_readme_the_prompt_and_the_pdf_name_the_sources_and_the_apache_attribution and `tests/test_ks_fact_schedule.py` test_ks_an_expired_fact_stays_in_the_build_marked_recheck_due.
- B-090, DMZ 1 follow-up, ask 2026-11-26-tcp-6edq-2 (a German docx of benchmark results came out over-wiped): a learned form never matches a word of `rules/known-words-de.txt`, `known-words.txt` or `common-words.txt` or a product or vendor name (`rules/vendor-names.txt`, `source-platforms.txt`, `tech-terms.txt`; a first name of the list stays learnable) and the German nouns Lauf, Durchlauf, Durchgang, Ansicht, Arbeiten, Mehrere, Grafikkarte and more are in `rules/known-words-de.txt`; in a German text the run of capitalised words needs a first name of `rules/first-names.txt` in it (a run that opens with Stadt, Landkreis and the like is still a name); a learned form matches whole words only (a letter of any script, a digit or a hyphen next to it is part of a word, so Grafikkarte is no `Grafik[name 3]`; the self-test case of a name in a host reads `srv_NAME_02` now); a BIC needs a country code of ISO 3166 at positions 5 and 6 and no dictionary word (a chapter title in capitals is none); in a docx only a heading style makes a heading (by its name or outline level in styles.xml), a picture alone leaves no empty `#` line, an empty list item or heading of an odt leaves none and a file with text says "N embedded image(s), not read as text" instead of "no text layer, review the original"; the table of `intake-report.md` uses the class words of the `wiped:` line of the import (person, company, place, name; a registered form is `registered`); `awb images release CUST FILEID N... [--project CODE]` puts the pictures into `input/FILEID-img/` of the project and the take note names the folder (the commit gate of a project checks no content since B-087, so they can be committed).
- B-089, DMZ 5 (the owner's word of 2026-11-25, "Согласен полностью": the checks stay at the entry and at the exit): the documentation pages, the knowledge base, the review, the lab logs and the files a session writes run no content check inside. `awb doc fetch` shows a public page whole (no withholding, no masking of a sample password; only a control character is refused). `awb kb add`, `amend`, `recheck`, `retire`, `merge`, `renew`, `how`, `verify`, `find`, `show`, `scope`, `expired`, the index, `awb kb pattern` and `awb kb import` check no name, structured data, code or secret and need no register (English, price, negative, duplicate and tag stay); the check moved to the exit: `kb.export` (`awb kb export` and `awb dataset build`) runs the new `kb.exit_screen` on every fact (registered name, secret, private key, token, home path, blocklist, code) and leaves a fact with a finding out and counts it (`left out: withheld N`), as it does for third-party products. `awb review init`, `claims`, `l0` and `pass` (no name hits, no rename hint), `awb voice learn DRAFT SENT`, `awb pdf`, `awb docx`, the price book, `awb refresh`, `awb ui submit` and `watch`, `awb codex run` (the `Paths:` and `Acceptance:` lines stay) check no content. Lab client output, lab run evidence and `awb winlab` output show a name as written; the shortening of key service secrets and the masking of held tenant keys stay. The codes file, the KPI file and the path inventory (`awb report --codes`, `awb files inventory`, `plan`), the owner status mail (it is sent also when the name check cannot run, `ownerwait.check_ran` is gone: it takes back the mail check of B-088 run 1), the owner status file, `awb ask` and the portal chat, `awb write translate`, `awb xchg put`, `awb bucket put` and the object names check no content (the uploaded copy is the file byte for byte). The solutions release, the dataset and `awb web publish` keep their checks and drop the identifier class (`ledger._detectors`; an address, a host name and a URL are no hit of `projectfacts.export_hits`). Tests: tests/test_dmz_outputs.py (7 tests, all fail on the old code); existing tests that asserted an inside check assert the new behaviour with their names kept, listed in the commit message.
- B-088, DMZ 4 (the owner's word of 2026-11-25, "Согласен полностью": the checks stay at the entry and at the exit, everything inside runs no content check): the ledger, the notes, the asks, the projects, the side sessions and the console check no content. A registered name, an IP address, a host name, a URL, a UUID, a mail or a secret shaped value is written and shown as written in: `awb ledger add` and its list and reports (`awb report --by`, `--period`), `awb career add` and `update`, `awb open sweep`, the rows of `awb queue`, the English note, `awb dev ask`, `awb desk ask`, `awb ux note`, the owner word, the hand-over, the notes of development (`awb projects note`, also held with `--after`), `awb projects list`, the waiting list and the daily mail of `awb owner status`, the goal, the tags and the park reason of `awb spawn` and `awb start`, the brief and the result of `awb side start` and `awb side take`, the files of a project in the console (`checked` of the project read API returns the text as written and `redacted` false; a locked vault no longer answers 503 there) and the note of `awb tenant cost note` (its character set stays; the id and blocklist refusals are gone). What stays is the line format: a control character is refused or withheld where a line needs it, valid UTF-8, size and path limits, the shape of a code, a tag of `rules/tags.txt` and the checks of the exit. `ledger.screen`, `screen_fields`, `check_entry` and `withhold`, `career.check_entry`, `projectfacts.withhold`, `devnote._secret_check`, `check_ran` and the verdict file of `awb queue` (`queue-verdicts.tsv`, its key and register stamp) are gone; `ledger.report` and `period.report` return the text alone; `ensure_checkable`, `hit_indexes` and `_detectors` stay for `projectfacts.export_hits` (the codes file and the files inventory that leave the host). Decisions: a session id of the receipt log is kept as written when it has ordinary characters (it used the side check); a spawn without the name check module looks the customer code up in the register alone. Tests changed to the new behaviour (names kept): tests/test_ledger.py (test_a_fixture_form_in_a_text_is_refused_without_echo, test_structured_data_and_blocked_words_are_refused, test_a_name_check_that_cannot_run_refuses_the_entry, test_a_vault_folder_without_a_register_refuses, test_a_text_with_a_name_registered_later_is_withheld, test_a_ledger_line_with_a_name_shaped_candidate_is_refused_with_a_reword_hint, test_review_check_errors_never_echo_dependency_values[screen], test_review_check_class_is_not_untrusted_output, test_review_detectors_fail_closed, test_review_secret_views_are_refused_on_add, test_review_stored_private_fields_never_reach_list_or_report, test_review_withheld_tags_do_not_duplicate_report_rows); tests/test_owner_page_decisions.py (test_names_and_unavailable_checks_never_offer_a_default, test_app_line_is_checked_and_never_guessed); tests/test_ownerwait_decisions.py (test_kept_tenant_and_location_are_screened_before_rendering, test_truncated_failure_reasons_are_screened_in_full, test_truncated_queue_text_is_screened_in_full, test_mail_refuses_if_check_becomes_unavailable_after_probe, test_lab_and_tenant_full_reasons_are_screened); tests/test_dn_notes_html.py::test_a_note_with_a_secret_or_without_a_title_is_refused; tests/test_projects_pick.py::test_a_next_line_with_a_registered_name_is_withheld; tests/test_k15_owner_mail.py::test_an_item_with_a_name_is_not_mailed; tests/test_lifecycle.py::test_the_english_note_of_the_last_reply_is_kept_once_and_never_with_a_name; tests/test_owner_take_default.py::test_unsafe_item_is_refused_without_repeating_its_text; tests/test_note_after.py::test_invalid_commit_and_secret_are_refused_without_output_values; tests/test_ux_events.py (test_awb_ux_note_refuses_a_registered_name_and_writes_nothing, test_the_prompt_hook_makes_no_vault_call_for_the_count); tests/test_side.py (test_take_does_not_copy_secrets_or_print_commit_values, test_names_are_checked_in_session_labels_and_git_output, test_output_checks_fail_closed_without_echoing_errors, test_git_filenames_are_checked_whole_and_controls_withheld, test_missing_name_register_withholds_output_and_refuses_start);
